# Memory

- Current round: closed the Vue 3 SFC `compileScript` mapped props type projection slice for `defineProps`.
- `vuec_sfc::compile_script` now resolves supported statically-expandable TypeScript mapped props types. The Rust SFC type resolver projects mapped type members, template-literal key remapping via ordered Cartesian expansion, string manipulation helpers (`Capitalize`, `Uppercase`, `Lowercase`, `Uncapitalize`), static `Extract` / `Exclude` over string key sets, `keyof` over resolvable props members in the ordered key path, and generic mapped type aliases with scoped type-parameter binding. This covers local examples such as ``[K in `_${Flag}_${Breakpoints}_`]``, key remapping such as ``[K in Flag as `${K}_flag`]``, and generic aliases such as `BreakpointFactory<'cols', number>`.
- External generic mapped props aliases now preserve their same-file static string key helper aliases when imported through the Vue 3 SFC type context, so `vuec_node_bridge` can resolve `import type { Props } from './types'; defineProps<Props<'cols', number>>()` and return normalized `compileScript().deps`. Ordered string literal declarations are now included when seeding external type deps. This remains a bounded Rust SFC type-projection slice: it does not implement arbitrary conditional/mapped type evaluation, TypeScript checker integration, recursive generic type solving beyond the scoped alias body path, unsupported/cyclic diagnostics, tsconfig/path/project references, default expression/function exports, or full Rust-backed SFC completion. No `xtask/src/compat.rs` change was made, no generated `target` output was edited, and no AST/HIR/MIR structural contracts changed.

Ambient `.d.ts` declarations (`declare interface` / `declare type` / `declare enum` / `declare namespace`) and module-style `export {}; declare global { ... }` files can now drive `defineProps`, `defineEmits`, and `defineModel` runtime inference. Static imports inside global type files are usable while resolving those global declarations, but imported names are not exposed as ambient globals. `compileScript().deps` records only global files and imported leaf type files that are actually used by macro type arguments, using normalized forward-slash paths.
- Public option parsing now flows through `vuec_node_bridge`, NAPI, WASM, and CLI `compile-sfc --global-type-file PATH`. This remains a bounded Rust SFC type-resolution slice: it implements explicit `globalTypeFiles` only, not tsconfig/path/project-reference resolution, automatic global type discovery, full NodeNext conditional exports, default expression/class/function exports, or full official `resolveType`. No `xtask/src/compat.rs` change was made, no generated `target` output was edited, and no AST/HIR/MIR structural contracts changed.

- Current round: closed the Vue 3 SFC `compileScript` TypeScript enum runtime type resolution and `deps` slice.
- `vuec_sfc::compile_script` now registers local and exported TypeScript enum declarations in the Vue 3 SFC type context before resolving other type declarations, so enum references can appear before their declaration and can flow through local, relative external, re-exported, namespace, and qualified type paths. Runtime inference follows the official enum boundary: string literal members produce `String`, numeric literal members produce `Number`, mixed enums produce `[String, Number]`, and enums with no recognizable string/numeric initializer fall back to `Number`. These runtime types feed `defineProps` prop members and `defineModel` union inference, and `compileScript().deps` records used external/re-exported/namespace enum source files through `vuec_node_bridge`.

- Current round: closed the Vue 3 SFC `compileScript().imports` public result-shape slice.
- `vuec_sfc::SfcScriptBlock.imports` now projects the Vue 3 public object shape keyed by local binding name instead of a source-string list. Each binding carries official `ImportBinding` fields: `isType`, `imported`, `local`, `source`, `isFromSetup`, and `isUsedInTemplate`. The Rust `compileScript` setup path records both normal `<script>` and `<script setup>` imports, omits Vue compiler macro imports such as `defineProps`, and follows the official template-usage boundary: inline templates mark imports unused, non-TS paths keep the broad `true`, and TS same-file non-`src`/non-`lang` templates use exact identifier usage scanning.
- `vuec_node_bridge` now serializes the same object through `sfc.compileScript`. - `vuec_sfc::compile_script` now consumes `SfcScriptCompileOptions.inline_template_ssr` for Vue 3 `<script setup>` inline templates. This is Rust SFC/SSR compiler behavior, not alias shim semantics. No `xtask/src/compat.rs` change was made, and no AST/HIR/MIR structural contracts changed.

- `vuec_vue3_core` now exposes a Rust-backed `stringifyStatic` public AST projection for official DOM transform-hoist callers. The selected default scope is intentionally entry-level and is reused by imported CSS Modules dependencies, matching the official loader plugin construction model; explicit `:local(...)` remains scoped/exported. `vuec_sfc`, `vuec_node_bridge`, `vuec_napi`, and `vuec_wasm` already forward `modulesOptions`, so the option is available through the public Rust-backed style paths. This does not claim RegExp object transport, invalid-pattern error parity, JavaScript `resolve` / `Loader` callbacks, or full PostCSS Modules option parity. No `xtask/src/compat.rs` alias shim semantics changed.

- Current round: closed the CSS Modules static package exports lookup slice.
- `vuec_style` now extends its Rust default CSS Modules file-system loader to honor static package `exports` entries for package roots and exact subpaths. The covered Stylus subset includes assignment variables, bare and `$name` variable replacement, nested selector flattening with `&`, selector-list cross product flattening, nested container at-rules, string-valued `preprocessOptions.additionalData`, filename-relative local `@import` inlining, `preprocessOptions.loadPaths` / `includePaths`, and normalized returned dependencies. CSS/HTTP/url imports remain CSS imports. This is a Rust SFC/style preprocessor slice; it does not claim full Stylus language, function/mixin, or PostCSS/lightningcss ecosystem parity, and no `xtask/src/compat.rs` alias shim semantics changed.

- Current round: closed the Rust SCSS/Sass style preprocessor slice.
- `vuec_style` now uses the Rust `grass` Sass compiler for `preprocessLang: "scss"` and `"sass"` instead of the previous hand-written variable/indentation shortcut. The Rust path supports real nested Sass compilation, `filename`-relative `@import` resolution through a virtual entry file system, normalized resolved Sass dependencies, and string-valued `preprocessOptions.additionalData`. `vuec_sfc`, the node bridge, NAPI, WASM, and native loaders now forward `preprocessOptions`; JavaScript package/`xtask` alias changes only evaluate function-valued `additionalData(source, filename)` before crossing the JSON/NAPI bridge because JavaScript functions cannot be serialized into Rust. - Current round: closed the Rust scoped `:deep()` selector rewrite slice.
- `vuec_style` now rewrites nested `:deep(...)` / `::v-deep(...)` inside top-level `:is()` / `:where()` / `:not()` / `:has()` containers like the official scoped-style plugin. This includes recursive scope injection inside single-branch containers and splitting mixed `:is()` / `:where()` / `:has()` branches so deep and non-deep branches receive the correct scope target. Existing deprecated `>>>`, `/deep/`, and combinator `::v-deep` behavior remains intact. This is Rust style compiler behavior; no `xtask/src/compat.rs` alias shim semantics changed.

Vue 2.7 SFC/script/style paths keep the official legacy underscore replacement and block-comment-only collection semantics. - Current round: closed the Rust scoped `:global()` selector rewrite slice.
- `vuec_style` now treats `:global(...)` / `::v-global(...)` like the official scoped-style plugin: only top-level selector pseudos replace the whole scoped selector with the first inner selector branch, while nested `:global(...)` inside container pseudos such as `:is()`, `:where()`, `:not()`, and `:has()` remains on the normal scoped path. This removes the previous over-eager Rust rewrite that incorrectly turned nested container pseudos into global selectors. This is Rust style compiler behavior; no `xtask/src/compat.rs` alias shim semantics changed.

- Current round: closed the `vuec_js` / Oxc integration slice.

The crate also owns Vue 3 invalid-expression diagnostic mapping from Oxc diagnostics, template-local to absolute span mapping through `TemplateJsSource` / `SourceAnchor`, a shared identifier prefix helper, and a structured Vue 2 filter pre-parser that validates the base expression with Oxc before rewriting filters to `_f(...)`. Vue 3 core invalid-expression diagnostics and Vue 2 filter codegen now reuse the shared `vuec_js` APIs. This is Rust compiler JS infrastructure only; no `xtask/src/compat.rs` alias/shim code or conformance classification changed.

- Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue3-core` improves from `95/214` to `126/214`; `parse.spec.ts` improves from `52/151` to `83/151`, while `compile.spec.ts` remains `3/3` and `scopeId.spec.ts` remains `4/4`. This is NAPI package/API projection work around Rust-backed parser/codegen entries; remaining Vue 3 core NAPI failures include real parser diagnostics/options, utility, `v-once`, and codegen parity gaps. `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed.

- Current round: closed the NAPI-backed Vue 2.7 SFC compileScript error propagation slice and full focused Vue 2.7 SFC NAPI conformance.
- The Vue 2.7 `vue/compiler-sfc` NAPI alias now throws when Rust-backed `compileVue27SfcScript` returns compile errors, matching public `compileScript` behavior for lang mismatches, invalid named exports, invalid macro argument combinations, local macro references, and unsupported `defineEmits` union types.
- Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` improves from `139/144` to `144/144`. Coverage remains honest: `compileScript`, `compileTemplate`, `parseComponent`, `cssVars`, `prefixIdentifiers`, `rewriteDefault`, and `stylePluginScoped` are `rust-backed`; `compileStyle` is `mixed` because caller-provided PostCSS callbacks/options execute in the JavaScript package adapter. `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed.

- Current round: closed the NAPI-backed Vue 2.7 SFC public script-setup marker projection slice.
- The Vue 2.7 `vue/compiler-sfc` NAPI alias now mirrors the generated public conformance adapter by setting `__vuecEmitScriptSetupMarker: false` when the official test environment defines `__TEST__ === true`, while leaving the Rust compiler default internal `__sfc` marker intact outside that public test projection.
- Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` improves from `77/144` to `139/144`; `compileScript.spec.ts` improves to `72/77`, `cssVars.spec.ts` is now `13/13`, and the remaining 5 Vue 2.7 SFC NAPI failures are isolated to `compileScript` error throwing. This is package/API option projection around Rust-backed script compilation; `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed.

- Current round: closed the NAPI-backed Vue 2.7 SFC compileStyle PostCSS callback boundary slice.
- The Vue 2.7 `vue/compiler-sfc` NAPI alias now strips caller-provided `postcssPlugins` / `postcssOptions` before crossing NAPI, runs Rust-backed style compilation first, then applies PostCSS in the JavaScript package adapter for sync and async public APIs. This preserves function-valued plugin callbacks/options that cannot be serialized into Rust.
- Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` improves from `73/144` to `77/144`; `compileStyle.spec.ts` is now `10/10` and remains correctly classified as `mixed`. `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed.

- Current round: closed the NAPI-backed Vue 2.7 SFC scoped-style default slice.
- The Vue 2.7 `vue/compiler-sfc` NAPI alias now applies the official `compileStyle` default of `scoped: true` when callers omit the option, while still preserving an explicit `scoped: false`. `vuec_napi` also treats a provided `map` option as a source-map request for style compilation.
- Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` improves from `63/144` to `73/144`; `stylePluginScoped.spec.ts` is now `5/5`, `compileStyle.spec.ts` is `6/10`, and CSS vars coverage improves to `8/13`. This is package/API option projection plus NAPI option parsing; scoped selector rewriting remains in Rust `vuec_style`, and `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed.

- Current round: closed the NAPI-backed Vue 2.7 SFC `compileTemplate` asset URL/srcset conformance slice.
- `vuec_napi` now maps Vue 2.7 SFC `compileTemplate` `transformAssetUrls` and `transformAssetUrlsOptions` into Rust `Vue2SfcAssetUrlTransformOptions`, including default/custom tag maps, `base`, `includeAbsolute`, URI fragments, empty `~`, and `srcset` handling through the existing Rust Vue 2 template compiler path.
- Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` improves from `57/144` to `63/144`; `compileTemplate.spec.ts` is now `9/9`. This is NAPI option parsing/routing only; template asset semantics remain in Rust `vuec_vue2`, and `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed.

- Current round: closed the NAPI-backed Vue 2.7 SFC `parseComponent` official conformance slice.
- `vuec_napi` now exposes `parseVue27SfcComponent`, routing public Vue 2.7 SFC parsing through Rust `SfcCompiler::parse_vue27_component_with_filename` so `pad`, `deindent`, source-range errors, special text template blocks, custom blocks, and top-level block projection use the Vue 2.7 parseComponent semantics instead of the generic Vue 3-style SFC parser.
- The local `@vuec-rs/native` loader and `vue/compiler-sfc` NAPI alias now use this Rust-backed entry for `parseComponent(source, options)`. Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` improves from `51/144` to `57/144`; `parseComponent.spec.ts` is now `11/11`. This is NAPI ABI and package/API routing for an existing Rust parser path; no `xtask/src/compat.rs` changes were made.

- Current round: closed the NAPI-backed Vue 2.7 compiler codeframe conformance gap.
- The Vue 2.7 `vue-template-compiler` NAPI alias now keeps the official one-argument `generateCodeFrame.length` API shape while reading optional `start` / `end` from `arguments` and forwarding them to the Rust-backed NAPI codeframe implementation.
- Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue27-compiler` now passes `190/190` official tests. `cargo xtask verify-napi-api` remains `7/7`, confirming the public function arity did not drift. This is package/API adapter argument forwarding only; codeframe formatting remains in Rust, and `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed.

- Current round: closed the NAPI-backed Vue 2.6 compiler official conformance warning-emission gap.

- Focused `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue2-compiler` now passes `188/188` official tests through the NAPI-backed official package-name alias. - Current round: improved the NAPI-backed Vue 2.6 official conformance runner path.

The `@vuec-rs/native` loader JSON-dehydrates payloads/options before crossing NAPI, and the NAPI `vue-template-compiler` aliases expose a non-enumerable `__vuecRuntime.callBridge` only for prepared official source shims.

- Current round: fixed the SFC scoped style brace-spacing option-matrix regression.
- `vuec_style` now preserves the original whitespace between a rewritten scoped selector and its `{` delimiter instead of forcing a space. Compact CSS such as `.a{...}` now stays compact as `.a[data-v-...]{...}`, while `.a {...}` keeps the selector-space form required by the Vue 2.7 official scoped-style specs. The fix is in the Rust style scanner; no `xtask/src/compat.rs` shim or JS post-processing was added.

- Added `cargo xtask verify-public-api-docs`, which currently runs rustdoc with `RUSTDOCFLAGS=-D missing_docs` for those documented foundation crates. This is partial public API documentation progress only; the global M20 "every public API has docs" criterion remains open until the rest of the public crates are covered.

- Current round: added the M20 release install-smoke gate slice.
- Added `cargo xtask verify-release-install-smoke`, which packs release-built npm artifacts, installs them into clean temporary projects, smoke-calls `@vuec-rs/native` via the current optional platform package, and smoke-calls `@vuec-rs/wasm` through the package default entry.
- Fixed `packages/wasm/index.js` so installed Node consumers default to `pkg-node/vuec_wasm.js` while browser-like runtimes continue to default to `pkg/vuec_wasm.js`. The gate currently reports `pending` rather than `pass` because non-current native platform packages still need matching target-platform release artifacts and host install smoke runs. This is release/package verification only; it does not change compiler semantics, `xtask/src/compat.rs`, conformance classification, or AST/HIR/MIR structures.

- Current round: added the M20 release dry-run gate slice.

- The gate currently reports `pending` rather than `pass`: non-current native platform npm packages need target-platform `vuec_napi.node` release artifacts, and first-time crates.io dry-runs for crates with internal path dependencies require their dependency crates to exist in the registry first. This is release verification infrastructure only; it does not change compiler semantics, `xtask/src/compat.rs`, conformance classification, or AST/HIR/MIR structures.

- Current round: completed the M19 string interning slice.
- Added internal string interning to `vuec_js::JsAstStore` for registered JS expression / statement / pattern / program source text. Repeated JS source text shares an `Arc<str>` backing inside the side store, while `JsEntry` still serializes `source` as a plain JSON string and AST/HIR/MIR nodes still carry the `JsExprId` / `JsStmtId` / `JsPatternId` handles required by `docs/3.AST_HIR_MIR_DESIGN.md`.
- Added `JsStringInternerStats` and `cargo xtask verify-string-interning`, which verifies cross-bucket reuse, distinct-source separation, serialized string output, and Vue3 DOM MIR codegen consuming the interned `JsAstStore`.

- Current round: completed the M19 arena allocation optimization slice.
- Added `AstDocument::with_capacity`, `reserve_nodes`, and `node_capacity`, plus shared template node-capacity hinting used by Vue 3 `base_parse` and Vue 2 public AST projection. This keeps `AstDocument<K>` as the single internal arena tree container and does not change AST/HIR/MIR node schemas, public projection, lowering, or target-split MIR semantics.
- Added `cargo xtask verify-arena`, which verifies requested arena capacity preserves deterministic `NodeId` allocation and tree invariants, and that representative Vue 2 / Vue 3 parser entrypoints preallocate from the shared hint while `validate_tree` still passes.

This is not used as arena validation evidence; focused Vue2 template, Vue2.7 template, and Vue3 DOM official conformance/output contracts passed for this allocation-only slice.

- Current round: completed the M19 Vue3 DOM AST-cache slice.
- Added `vuec_vue3_dom::DomCompiler`, an incremental DOM compiler facade that caches parsed and DOM-normalized `Vue3Ast` values by filename, source hash, source identity, base offset, and parse/DOM-normalization options. The cache returns a cloned AST per compile before transform/codegen, so AST mutations from transform/codegen do not leak back into cached state.
- Added `DomAstCacheStats` and `cargo xtask verify-ast-cache`, which verifies unchanged-input hits, changed same-file invalidation, and separate parse-option cache entries through public Rust APIs. This does not change AST/HIR/MIR structures, public projection rules, lowering rules, or target-split MIR semantics.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check -p vuec_vue3_dom -p vuec_sfc -p xtask`, `cargo test -p vuec_vue3_dom --lib`, `cargo test -p xtask`, `cargo xtask verify-ast-cache`, `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue3-dom` (`133/133`), `cargo xtask run-output-contract --version-line vue3 --package @vue/compiler-dom` (`5/5`), `cargo xtask bench --iterations 1 --skip-official-js`, and `git diff --check`.
- Broader `cargo xtask summarize-compat --locked` currently fails because the `vue2_7::vue/compiler-sfc` conformance artifact reports `73/144` passing tests, concentrated in existing Vue2.7 SFC compileScript/cssVars/stylePluginScoped areas. This was not used as AST-cache validation evidence; focused Vue3 DOM conformance/output-contract passed for this slice.

- Current round: completed the M19 parallel compilation slice.
- Added `vuec compile-batch`, which accepts multiple inputs for Vue 2 template, Vue 3 template, Vue 3 SFC, or Vue 3 SSR compilation, runs independent files through a bounded worker pool, and preserves deterministic input-order JSON results. Each worker uses the existing compiler crates directly; no compiler semantics were moved into `xtask/src/compat.rs`, and AST/HIR/MIR structures were not changed.
- Added `cargo xtask verify-parallel`, which builds the real `vuec` binary and verifies batch compilation for Vue 2 template, Vue 3 template, Vue 3 SFC, and Vue 3 SSR targets.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check -p vuec_cli -p xtask`, `cargo test -p vuec_cli`, `cargo test -p xtask`, `cargo xtask verify-parallel`, and `git diff --check`.

- Current round: completed the M19 benchmark memory-peak reporting and conformance-regression evidence slice.
- `cargo xtask bench` now records a best-effort `peakRssBytes` field for Rust CLI and locked official JS benchmark child processes using `sysinfo` process sampling. The report keeps the field nullable for very short-lived processes or unsupported sampling windows, but the gate now captures real RSS values on this Windows environment for the representative benchmark cases.
- Verified current M19 cache/reporting changes against compatibility summary with `cargo xtask summarize-compat --locked` (`7/7` pass). This is regression evidence for the completed M19 performance/incremental infrastructure; arena/string/AST-cache optimization remained open at that point.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check -p xtask`, `cargo test -p xtask`, `cargo xtask bench --iterations 1`, `cargo xtask summarize-compat --locked`, and `git diff --check`.

- Current round: completed the M19 SFC descriptor-cache / incremental invalidation slice.
- Added an in-memory SFC descriptor cache inside `vuec_sfc::SfcCompiler`, keyed by filename, source hash, and parse mode. Repeated same-file/same-source parses reuse the descriptor; same-file changed-source parses invalidate stale entries before rebuilding the descriptor. Cache stats expose descriptor hits, misses, and invalidations for tests and gates.
- Added `cargo xtask verify-incremental`, which verifies same-file unchanged input hits the SFC descriptor cache, changed same-file input invalidates it, and the Vue 2.7 parse mode has its own cache hit path. This gate calls `vuec_sfc` public APIs directly; no compiler semantics were moved into `xtask/src/compat.rs`, and AST/HIR/MIR structures were not changed.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check -p vuec_sfc -p xtask`, `cargo test -p vuec_sfc --lib`, `cargo test -p xtask`, `cargo xtask verify-incremental`, and `git diff --check`.

- Current round: completed the first M19 benchmark-report framework slice.
- Added `cargo xtask bench`, which writes stable benchmark fixtures under `target/bench/fixtures`, runs the real `vuec` CLI benchmark path for Vue 2 template, Vue 3 template, Vue 3 SFC, and Vue 3 SSR cases, and writes `target/bench/bench-report.json` with fixture hashes, git commit/dirty state, OS/arch, Rust/Node/npm/pnpm versions, lock hash, iterations, and per-case timings.
- The same gate prepares locked official npm compilers from `compat/official-revisions.lock` under `target/bench/official-js` and records official JS compiler timings for the same fixture set. On Windows the xtask program resolver now prefers spawnable `.exe/.cmd/.bat/.com` shims so npm-based probes do not accidentally select non-executable fnm shims.
- This is benchmark/reporting infrastructure only. It does not change compiler semantics, AST/HIR/MIR structure, conformance classification, cache behavior, or `xtask/src/compat.rs`.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo test -p xtask`, `cargo xtask bench --iterations 1`, and `git diff --check`.

- Current round: completed the M17 WASI smoke slice.

It performs I/O and command dispatch only; compiler semantics remain in the Rust compiler crates.

- Current round: completed the M17 WASM recoverable error-conversion slice.
- `vuec_wasm` exported JSON-string functions now strictly parse options JSON instead of silently falling back to defaults. Invalid options JSON and serialization failures return structured `errors` plus `diagnostics` objects with stable `VUEC_WASM_*` codes. Unwind-capable builds also convert caught boundary panics to the same envelope; wasm32 remains `panic=abort`, with panic visibility handled by the existing console hook.
- The Node wasm-bindgen smoke now imports the raw generated wasm module in addition to the JS loader and verifies invalid-options error conversion directly at the ABI boundary.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check -p vuec_wasm -p xtask`, `cargo test -p vuec_wasm -p xtask`, `cargo xtask verify-wasm`, and `git diff --check`.

- Current round: completed the M17 WASM runtime initialization slice.

- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo check -p vuec_wasm -p xtask`, `cargo test -p vuec_wasm -p xtask`, `cargo xtask verify-wasm`, and `git diff --check`.

The crate delegates to existing Rust compiler crates; no compiler semantics were moved into JS or `xtask`.

- Current round: added `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance` as the official-test execution backend for NAPI-backed official package-name aliases.
- `run_conformance` now selects an alias backend internally: the existing command preserves generated Rust alias behavior and report names, while `run-napi-conformance` prepares version-isolated NAPI alias roots, runs the same prepared Jasmine/Vitest official suites against those package names, writes `napi-*.json` reports, and records `alias_backend: "napi"` in each report.
- NAPI conformance reports are intentionally classified as `mixed` harness coverage because prepared source shims, official TypeScript source, and JavaScript-only adapters can still participate. The command does not add compiler semantics to `xtask/src/compat.rs` and does not claim pure Rust/NAPI semantic coverage.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo test -p xtask`, `cargo xtask --help` showing `run-napi-conformance`, `cargo xtask run-napi-conformance --suite vue2-compiler` (runner executes and honestly fails with `14/188` passed, `174` failed, writing `target/conformance/ee33465b421a58b83fac04aa850a6d250ee09ec169fa80ed8820794a0c9a2769/napi-vue2-compiler.json`), and `git diff --check`.

- Current round: closed the remaining Vue 3 `@vue/compiler-sfc` NAPI public option gap and made the full NAPI public option matrix pass.
- `vuec_napi` now exposes `compileSfcTemplateSource`, which routes Vue 3 SFC public `compileTemplate({ source, ...options })` through Rust `vuec_sfc::compile_template_source` with the official default source-map filename `template.vue.html`. This preserves whole-source compilation, side-effect `<script>` / `<style>` diagnostics, and full-source source maps in Rust instead of extracting the inner template in JavaScript.
- The local `@vuec-rs/native` loader now uses `compileSfcTemplateSource` for the public `compileTemplate(options)` facade while keeping `compileSfcTemplate(source, options)` as the lower-level descriptor/template-block compiler entry.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_napi -p vuec_sfc`, `cargo xtask verify-napi`, `cargo xtask verify-napi-alias`, `cargo xtask verify-napi-api` (`7/7`), `cargo xtask run-napi-option-matrix --version-line vue3 --package @vue/compiler-sfc` (`4/4`), `cargo xtask run-napi-option-matrix --all` (`7/7`), `cargo xtask run-napi-output-contract --all` (`7/7`), and `git diff --check`.

- Current round: closed the Vue 2.7 `vue/compiler-sfc` NAPI public option parity slice for the NAPI-backed public contract backend.
- `vuec_napi` now exposes Vue 2.7-specific SFC template/script NAPI entry points. `compileVue27SfcTemplate` keeps template preprocessing and Vue 2 render generation in Rust while projecting the official Vue 2.7 public wrapper shape; `compileVue27SfcScript` routes full SFC source through Rust `vuec_sfc::compile_vue27_script`.
- The NAPI `vue/compiler-sfc` alias now projects Vue 2.7 descriptor/script/style public shapes at the package boundary: descriptor block content offsets, `scriptSetup` / `customBlocks` naming, CSS vars, style dependency elision, non-enumerable `bindings.__isScriptSetup`, and public script `start` / `end` fields. Compiler semantics remain in Rust crates; the JavaScript alias is API projection glue.
- Rust Vue 2.7 script setup codegen now returns the official public marker shape `return { __sfc: true,... }`, including empty-return cases, matching Vue 2.7 `compileScript` output instead of only satisfying the option fixture.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all -- --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_sfc -p vuec_napi -p vuec_style`, `cargo xtask verify-napi`, `cargo xtask verify-napi-alias`, `cargo xtask run-napi-output-contract --version-line vue2_7 --package vue --entry vue/compiler-sfc` (`5/5`), and `cargo xtask run-napi-option-matrix --version-line vue2_7 --package vue --entry vue/compiler-sfc` (`4/4`). At the time this slice landed, full `cargo xtask run-napi-option-matrix --all` reached `6/7` targets passing, with only Vue 3 `@vue/compiler-sfc` `compileTemplate:vue3-sfc-template` still open.

- Current round: closed the Vue 2 `vue-template-compiler` NAPI public option parity slice for the NAPI-backed public contract backend.
- `vuec_napi` now parses sparse Vue 2 public compile options by starting from `Vue2CompileOptions::default()` and overlaying public camelCase/snake_case keys. This fixes NAPI handling for `comments`, custom `delimiters`, `whitespace: "condense"`, `preserveWhitespace`, `shouldDecodeNewlines*`, reserved/native metadata, and binding metadata instead of falling back to defaults when callers provide only one option key.
- The NAPI official `vue-template-compiler` aliases now project Vue 2 compile/SSR/compileToFunctions results into the official public result shape: `staticRenderFns`, string-array `errors` / `tips` when `outputSourceRange` is false, ranged `{ msg, start, end }` issue objects when enabled, and no public `diagnostics` / `static_render_fns` implementation fields. This is package/API adapter work at the public boundary; compiler option semantics remain in Rust `vuec_vue2`.
- Verification: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_napi`, `cargo xtask verify-napi`, `cargo xtask verify-napi-alias`, `cargo xtask verify-napi-api` (`7/7`), `cargo xtask run-napi-output-contract --all` (`7/7`), `cargo xtask run-napi-option-matrix --version-line vue2_6 --package vue-template-compiler` (`10/10`), and `cargo xtask run-napi-option-matrix --version-line vue2_7 --package vue-template-compiler` (`3/3`). Full `cargo xtask run-napi-option-matrix --all` was rerun and still intentionally not marked passing: current result is `5/7` targets pass, with failures only in Vue 2.7 SFC parse/template/script/style rows and Vue 3 SFC compileTemplate/compileStyle rows.

- Current round: added an M16 NAPI-backed public option/output contract verification backend.
- `cargo xtask run-napi-output-contract --all` now builds `vuec_napi`, prepares version-isolated NAPI official package-name alias roots under `target/compat/napi-alias/{vue2_6,vue2_7,vue3}`, selects the correct Vue 2.6 / Vue 2.7 `vue-template-compiler` public entry shape, and runs the existing official-vs-Rust output contract probes against NAPI aliases instead of the generated `vuec_node_bridge` alias tree. The command passes `7/7`.
- `cargo xtask run-napi-option-matrix` now has the same NAPI alias backend. Vue 3 public compiler options pass through NAPI for `@vue/compiler-core` (`8/8`), `@vue/compiler-dom` (`4/4`), and `@vue/compiler-ssr` (`2/2`). Full `--all` still fails on real Vue 2 and SFC option gaps, including Vue 2 comments/delimiters/whitespace/newline decode, Vue 2.7 SFC parse/template/script/style exact option rows, and Vue 3 SFC template/style source-map/style-format rows.
- Fixed NAPI package adapter gaps found by the new backend: `@vue/compiler-core.baseCompile` now raises official public option errors for unsupported `cacheHandlers` without prefix/module mode and `scopeId` outside module mode, and the Vue 2.7 `vue/compiler-sfc.compileTemplate` NAPI alias routes to the Vue 2 template compiler instead of the Vue 3 DOM compiler for public output-contract execution.
- `xtask/src/compat.rs` changes in this round are runner/package-backend support only: they prepare NAPI package trees and reuse existing option/output probe scripts. No compiler semantics were added to `xtask/src/compat.rs`, and full official source conformance remains on the generated alias runtime until the internal source-test imports are migrated to Rust-backed public/bridge APIs.

Normal scripts use Rust `rewriteDefault` plus `_useCssVars` setup wrapping, and script setup codegen injects `_useCssVars`, moves retained imports to module scope, analyzes setup/import/props bindings, and prefixes CSS var expressions through `_setup` or `_vm` using the Rust `prefixIdentifiers` implementation.

- Current Vue 2.7 SFC conformance movement: focused official `packages/compiler-sfc/test/cssVars.spec.ts` now passes `13/13`. Full `vue27-sfc` conformance improves from `35/144` to `48/144`, remains expected-failing with `96` Rust-backed failures, and coverage reports `rust-backed: 48 pass / 96 fail`, `mixed: 0`, `shim-backed: 0`.
- Verification for this SFC cssVars slice: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `git diff --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_style --lib`, `cargo test -p vuec_sfc --lib`, `cargo test -p vuec_node_bridge`, `cargo test -p xtask`, `cargo build -p vuec_node_bridge`, `cargo xtask verify-npm-alias --version-line vue2_7 --package vue/compiler-sfc`, focused official Vitest `cssVars.spec.ts` (`13/13`), `cargo xtask diff-api --version-line vue2_7 --package vue/compiler-sfc`, `cargo xtask run-output-contract --version-line vue2_7 --package vue/compiler-sfc`, and `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` as expected fail (`48/144`).

- Current round: completed the Vue 2.7 SFC `prefixIdentifiers` Rust rewrite/internal source-entry slice.
- `vuec_sfc` now implements Vue 2.7 `prefixIdentifiers(source, isFunctional?, isTS?, babelOptions?, bindings?)` behavior in Rust for render-function code: it parses Vue 2 render source with Oxc, removes `with(this)` blocks, injects `_vm` / `_c` / `_setup` declarations, prefixes non-local references through `_vm.` or `_setup.` for setup bindings, preserves function/v-for callback locals, and expands object shorthand properties such as `{ color }` to `{ color: _vm.color }`.
- `vuec_node_bridge` now exposes `sfc.vue27.prefixIdentifiers`. `xtask/src/compat.rs` only routes the internal `packages/compiler-sfc/src/prefixIdentifiers.ts` test/import entry to the Rust bridge, forwards public alias arguments, sets `VUEC_NODE_BRIDGE` for prepared runners, and points Vue 2.7 SFC tests at the Node Prettier entry; no compiler rewrite semantics were added to JavaScript shims.
- Current Vue 2.7 SFC conformance movement: focused official `packages/compiler-sfc/test/prefixIdentifiers.spec.ts` now passes `2/2`. Full `vue27-sfc` conformance improves from `33/144` to `35/144` and remains expected-failing with `109` Rust-backed failures in compileScript/style/template/cssVars/stylePluginScoped areas.
- Verification for this SFC prefix slice: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `git diff --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_sfc --lib`, `cargo test -p vuec_node_bridge`, `cargo test -p xtask`, `cargo build -p vuec_node_bridge`, `cargo xtask verify-npm-alias --version-line vue2_7 --package vue/compiler-sfc`, focused official Vitest `prefixIdentifiers.spec.ts` (`2/2`), `cargo xtask diff-api --version-line vue2_7 --package vue/compiler-sfc`, `cargo xtask run-output-contract --version-line vue2_7 --package vue/compiler-sfc`, and `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` as expected fail (`35/144`).

- Current round: completed the Vue 2.7 SFC `rewriteDefault` Rust AST rewrite/API slice.
- `vuec_sfc` now implements Vue 2.7 `rewriteDefault(input, as, parserPlugins?)` in Rust using Oxc module AST spans for `ExportDefaultDeclaration` and named default exports, plus source-edit application for default class/function/object expressions, `export { a as default }`, and re-export forms such as `export { default } from './x'` / `export { foo as default } from './x'`. The implementation preserves the official observable fast-path vs fallback whitespace behavior for decorated classes while still deriving the actual rewrite targets from parsed Rust AST spans.
- `vuec_node_bridge` now exposes `sfc.vue27.rewriteDefault` and parses official parser plugin hints into Rust rewrite options. `xtask/src/compat.rs` only routes the public export and arity/argument shape to the bridge; no compiler semantics were added to JavaScript shims.

- Current round: completed the Vue 2.7 SFC `parseComponent` Rust parser/API slice.
- `vuec_sfc` now has a top-level SFC block scanner for Vue 2.7 `parseComponent` semantics: it preserves arbitrary attributes as `string | true`, ignores nested `<style>` blocks inside normal template content, keeps custom blocks in source order, supports official default/deindent options, implements `pad: true | "line" | "space"` for non-template blocks, and recovers malformed trailing `</` with source-range errors.

- Current round: completed the Vue 2.7 compiler Rust binding-metadata codegen parity slice.

- Current round: completed the Vue 2.6 compiler conformance runner/import support closure.
- `xtask/src/compat.rs` now gives the Vue 2.6 Jasmine runner a deterministic `jsdom` DOM environment, disables Babel register cache for regenerated prepared suites, and uses a CommonJS default-import interop helper so `import Vue from 'vue'` binds to the Vue constructor when the official runtime exports via `module.exports`. Runner dependency resolution can now fall back to the same synced official vendor tree for runner infrastructure dependencies missing from the current baseline manifest; in this slice Vue 2.6 resolves `jsdom` from the Vue 2.7 official lock/manifest rather than from an unpinned latest version.
- This `xtask/src/compat.rs` work is runner/import support only. No compiler semantics were added to the shim, and AST/HIR/MIR structures did not change.

- Current round: completed a Vue 2 `outputSourceRange` diagnostic span parity slice.
- `vuec_vue2` now preserves raw attribute spans on `Vue2Attribute` instead of widening them to the element start tag, carries consumed `v-if` / `v-else-if` / `v-else` / `v-for` / `key` spans on `Vue2Element`, and uses those spans for invalid expression diagnostics and `<slot>` key warnings. Vue 2 public error/tip serialization now omits absent `start` / `end` fields, so text-only root errors can expose `start` without an `end` like the official compiler.
- `docs/3.AST_HIR_MIR_DESIGN.md` was updated because the new Vue 2 source-range fields are AST diagnostic contract data, not optimizer/codegen state. `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not touched in this slice.

This `xtask/src/compat.rs` work is API/hydration/option adapter support only; compiler/parser/optimizer semantics are in Rust, and no temporary compiler semantics were added to the shim.

- `vuec_html::HtmlTokenizer` now exposes its cursor so the Vue 2 parser can switch to raw-text consumption while keeping tokenizer ownership and source offsets deterministic.

Public compile results now also expose `staticRenderFns`.

This is API adapter support only, not compiler semantics; Rust compile behavior was already producing the same empty-root default through `generate_render(None, ...)`.

- `vuec_vue2` now generates `inlineTemplate:{render,staticRenderFns}` from the first element child, matching official Vue 2 behavior even when an inline-template component has multiple children. Empty inline-template components still omit inlineTemplate data, while invalid child counts now produce the official `Inline-template components must have exactly one child element.` compiler warning through Rust diagnostics.

This is API/runner warning adapter support only; compiler semantics remain in Rust, and parse-stage shims suppress only the codegen-stage inline-template warning to avoid double-emitting it during official `parse -> generate` tests.

- Current round: completed a Vue 2 compiler Rust scoped-slot codegen parity slice.
- `vuec_vue2` now performs Vue 2 parser-style final cleanup for direct scoped-slot children: slot-scope nodes are registered under the parent `scopedSlots` map but no longer also render as normal children. Scoped-slot elements now force data object emission where official codegen requires `_c('div',{},...)`.
- Scoped slot codegen now handles stable slots, dynamic slot names, force-update `_u(...,null,true)` metadata, legacy `slot-scope` + `v-if` function bodies, and new `v-slot` / `#` conditional slot object generation. Regression coverage was added for the official scoped-slot codegen cases.
- This is Rust compiler implementation work in `crates/vuec_vue2`; `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed, and no AST/HIR/MIR structure changed. The new `slot_new_syntax` field is an internal codegen marker skipped from serialization, not a public AST/HIR/MIR contract change.
- Current Vue 2 conformance movement: `vue2-compiler` improved from `72/188` to `77/188`, and `vue27-compiler` improved from `70/190` to `75/190`. `vue27-sfc` remains `5/144`, unchanged by this compiler scoped-slot codegen slice.
- Verification for this Vue 2 scoped-slot codegen slice: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `git diff --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_vue2 --lib` (`10/10`), `cargo build -p vuec_node_bridge`, `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue2-compiler` (expected fail, `77/188`), `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue27-compiler` (expected fail, `75/190`), and `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` (expected fail, unchanged `5/144`).

- Current round: completed a Vue 2 compiler Rust event-handler codegen parity slice.
- `vuec_vue2` now preserves source modifier order for Vue 2 event handlers, keeps a codegen-only marker for handlers that originally had a modifier object, and matches official wrapping behavior for `.capture`, `.once`, and `.passive` event-option modifiers. Event codegen now distinguishes method paths, function expressions, simple function invocations, and inline statements more closely to Vue 2 official `events.js`, so multi-statement handlers and string-heavy malformed calls no longer receive a spurious `return`.
- Vue 2 key/generic/mouse modifier generation now follows official order and guard semantics, including ordered chained key filters, ordered `.stop.prevent.self`, `.exact`, and mouse button guards. Regression coverage was added for the official event handler failures.
- This is Rust compiler implementation work in `crates/vuec_vue2`; `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed, and no AST/HIR/MIR structure changed.
- Current Vue 2 conformance movement: `vue2-compiler` improved from `64/188` to `72/188`, and `vue27-compiler` improved from `62/190` to `70/190`. `vue27-sfc` remains `5/144`, unchanged by this template-compiler event codegen slice.
- Verification for this Vue 2 event-handler codegen slice: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `git diff --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_vue2 --lib` (`9/9`), `cargo build -p vuec_node_bridge`, `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue2-compiler` (expected fail, `72/188`), `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue27-compiler` (expected fail, `70/190`), and `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue27-sfc` (expected fail, unchanged `5/144`).

- Current round: completed a Vue 2 compiler Rust codegen/data-object parity slice for refs and `v-model`.
- `vuec_vue2` now treats `ref`, `refInFor`, `model`, `pre`, inline-template, validation, listener/data wrappers, and other codegen-visible state as non-plain data-object triggers, so plain-looking elements that still need VNode data no longer drop generated data. DOM `v-model` no longer emits duplicate runtime directive metadata, and model assignment generation now follows official Vue 2 whitespace semantics by using the trimmed expression only for path parsing while preserving original expression whitespace for direct assignment output.
- Vue 2 props codegen now distinguishes static HTML attributes from JavaScript expression-valued DOM props/dynamic attrs, preserving newline/entity handling for real attrs while avoiding JS expression newline escaping in `domProps`.
- This is Rust compiler implementation work in `crates/vuec_vue2`; `xtask/src/compat.rs` was not changed, and no AST/HIR/MIR structure changed.
- Current Vue 2 conformance movement: `vue2-compiler` improved from `59/188` to `64/188`, and `vue27-compiler` improved from `57/190` to `62/190`. `vue27-sfc` remains `5/144`, unchanged by this template-compiler codegen slice.

This is runner/import adapter support only; no Vue 2 compiler semantics were added to `xtask/src/compat.rs`.

Vue 2 coverage reasons now explicitly state that the suites execute real official tests and that failures are real Rust parity gaps, not `not-wired` pending status.

The `optimize(ast)` shim is a no-op because Rust `compile(...)` already returns optimized output for public compile/codegen calls; any future internal AST mutation parity must move into Rust compiler/projection code, not grow semantic behavior in `xtask/src/compat.rs`.

- Current round: completed the Vue 3 SFC `compileTemplate` option-matrix static-cache / SSR void-tag parity slice.
- `vuec_vue3_core` now decides VNode static cache eligibility from the whole static subtree instead of only the child element's own props. Dynamic interpolation subtrees such as `<div>{{ msg }}</div>` now keep the official `TEXT` patch flag and are not wrapped in `_cache[n]` as `-1 /* CACHED */`, while constant static `v-bind` values remain cacheable for VNode caching.
- `vuec_sfc` now applies DOM parser defaults to both DOM and SSR template compile paths, so SFC SSR output uses the same void-tag behavior as the DOM parser defaults and no longer emits closing tags such as `</img>`. Template error merging now deduplicates duplicate side-effect tag diagnostics by code and source offsets.

- Current round: added Rust-backed Vue 3 exact render emitter `v-once` cache-wrapper codegen for public `baseCompile`.
- The exact emitter now emits official-shaped `_cache[n] || (...)` wrappers with `_setBlockTracking(-1, true)`, `.cacheIndex = n`, `_setBlockTracking(1)`, and final `_cache[n]` return for `v-once` nodes. Non-control-flow `v-once` renders vnode-root code, while `v-if` and `v-for` wrap the full generated branch-chain / render-list fragment expression.
- Cache slot allocation now keeps `v-once` distinct from `v-memo`, `v-for` memo render-list caches, cacheHandlers, and static child caches. Plain `v-once` allocates the once slot before nested static child caches; same-node `v-memo v-once` preserves Vue's memo-first order (`memo` slot before `once` slot); `v-for v-memo v-once` preserves the render-list memo slot plus reserved transformMemo slot before the once wrapper slot. Nested once rendering uses `RenderScope.in_v_once` to avoid nested block-tracking wrappers while still allowing static child caches inside the outer once body.

DOM MIR codegen preserves `v-for` aliases, destructured loop locals, stable/dynamic slot params, dynamic-slot `v-for` aliases, `$event`, and arrow handler params as locals while prefixing external reads such as `_ctx.msg`, `_ctx.list`, dynamic slot names, directive expressions, dynamic props, object listeners, dynamic components, and cached event handlers.
- This is structural target-codegen work and does not touch `xtask/src/compat.rs`. The exact AST emitter remains the official Vue 3 conformance path until complete MIR-driven exact codegen migration, but the previous standalone DOM MIR raw-expression gap is closed for the current MIR subset.

- `Vue3DomMirKind::For` now carries `Vue3ForMir`, including source, value/key/index aliases, explicit key expression/string, and optional `Vue3ForMemo { expression, index }`. DOM lowering records the key/memo target payload after lowering the loop body, keeping `JsAstStore` ownership stable for existing element props.
- `generate_vue3_dom_mir` now emits memoized render-list cache code from MIR: `__, ___, _cached` params, `const _memo`, optional key guard, `_isMemoSame`, `const _item`, `_item.memo`, `_cache` argument, and keyed fragment flag.

- Current round: added non-`v-for` `v-memo` wrappers to structural Vue 3 DOM MIR.
- `Vue3DomMirKind::Memo { expression, index }` now stores the memo expression as a `JsExprId` and the target cache slot. DOM lowering wraps non-`v-for` memoized elements before lowering their VNode body; `v-for` memo cache guards remain out of scope for this slice.
- `generate_vue3_dom_mir` now imports `_withMemo` and emits `_withMemo(expression, () => vnode, _cache, index)` from MIR. Structural directives such as `memo`, `once`, `slot`, `pre`, `html`, and `text` are excluded from runtime HIR directives.

- Current round: added dynamic component slots payloads to structural Vue 3 DOM MIR.

Dynamic entries cover static/dynamic slot names, optional slot params, conditional slot guards with generated keys, and slot `v-for` source / alias patterns. Slot params and loop expressions remain in `JsAstStore`; slot bodies remain child node IDs in the same `Vue3DomMir` document.
- DOM lowering now projects dynamic slot names, slot `v-if` / `v-else-if`, and slot `v-for` into `MirChildren::Slots` and sets `Vue3SlotFlag::Dynamic`; `generate_vue3_dom_mir` emits `_createSlots`, `_renderList`, dynamic slot objects, conditional `undefined` fallbacks, and `1024 /* DYNAMIC_SLOTS */` from MIR.

- Current round: added a stable component slots payload to structural Vue 3 DOM MIR.

DOM lowering projects default component children, static named `#slot` template children, and static on-component `v-slot` params into this payload, while leaving dynamic / conditional / loop slots for a later slice.

DOM lowering projects `<Child>`, `<Transition>`, and `<component :is="view">` into those target variants while reusing the existing `JsAstStore` expression for dynamic `is`.

The standalone MIR emitter now emits static attrs, dynamic bindings, event props, class normalization, runtime directive wrappers, directive asset declarations, and directive/runtime helpers from MIR.

The initial emitter covers the current structural MIR subset: root children, VNode calls, text/interpolation text VNodes, slot outlets, `v-if` comment fallbacks, `v-for` render-list wrappers, fragments, `v-once` cache wrappers, conservative hoist references, patch flags, dynamic prop names, and module-mode helper imports derived from MIR node kinds.

- Current round: migrated the Vue 3 exact render emitter's asset/helper selection away from legacy AST/code-string probing and onto transformed `Vue3Root` codegen state where available.
- `Vue3Dialect::generate` now consumes `Vue3Root.helpers`, `Vue3Root.components`, and `Vue3Root.directives` for helper imports/destructuring plus component/directive asset declarations, with raw-AST fallback scans preserved for direct public `generate(ast, options)` calls that have not run `transform`. Component rendering now uses runtime helper tags for built-in components, `_resolveDynamicComponent(...)` for dynamic `<component :is>`, and only emits `_resolveComponent(...)` declarations for collected component assets.

- Current round: completed the Vue 2 `render` / `staticRenderFns` migration from the compatibility `Vue2Element` tree to `Vue2Mir`.
- `vuec_ast::Vue2MirKind` now carries richer target payloads for create-element data objects, runtime directives, static render wrappers, `v-once`, slot outlets, scoped slots, validation data, inline templates, component model data, and filter call args. `Vue2Ast` projection now preserves codegen-observable slot syntax, validator metadata, event modifier order, and scoped slot node ids while keeping codegen state out of public AST.
- `vuec_vue2::compile` and `generate` now project to `Vue2Ast`, lower through `HIR` / `Vue2Mir`, and emit `render` / `staticRenderFns` from `Vue2Mir` plus `JsAstStore`. The old element-tree emitter was removed; static roots lower as `RenderStatic` wrappers whose body remains in the same MIR document, and scoped-slot / inline-template data is stored in `Vue2DataObject`.
- Added MIR structure coverage that asserts events, attrs, scoped slot payloads, and static render bodies exist in `Vue2Mir`, plus a regression for `v-if` template branches containing nested `v-for` component children requiring parent child normalization.
- Verification for this Vue 2 MIR codegen slice: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_vue2 --lib -- --nocapture` (`29/29`), `cargo check -p vuec_ast -p vuec_vue2 -p vuec_js -p vuec_codegen -p vuec_napi`, `cargo xtask run-option-matrix --version-line vue2_6 --package vue-template-compiler` (`10/10`), `cargo xtask run-output-contract --version-line vue2_6 --package vue-template-compiler` (`5/5`), `cargo xtask run-option-matrix --version-line vue2_7 --package vue-template-compiler` (`3/3`), `cargo xtask run-output-contract --version-line vue2_7 --package vue-template-compiler` (`5/5`), `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue2-compiler` (`188/188`), `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue27-compiler` (`190/190`), `git diff --check`, and focused TODO search over the touched code/docs files.

- Current round: completed the Rust-backed Vue 3 `compiler-core` parser recovery slice to official `parse.spec.ts` parity. Shared `vuec_html` now tokenizes invalid-space end tags, empty end tags, bogus question tags, and invalid `=`-prefixed attributes as observable markup boundaries instead of folding them into text.
- `vuec_vue3_core::base_parse` now matches official recovery for invalid/non-matching end tags, raw-text `textarea`/`title` content, complete vs incomplete empty end tags, bogus `<?...>` nodes, EOF comments, adjacent text merging across skipped bogus markup, and EOF start-tag recovery. Public projection handles empty interpolation inner locs, dynamic directive arguments missing `]`, and the official EOF trailing-solidus loc quirk at bridge projection time.
- `vuec_node_bridge` parse diagnostics now cover these recovery paths with official messages/locations, including `UNEXPECTED_EQUALS_SIGN_BEFORE_ATTRIBUTE_NAME`, `MISSING_END_TAG_NAME`, `UNEXPECTED_QUESTION_MARK_INSTEAD_OF_TAG_NAME`, invalid-space `X_INVALID_END_TAG`, EOF comment/tag, and dynamic directive argument recovery.
- `xtask/src/compat.rs` changes in this round are API/test-runner support only: generated Vue 3 aliases hydrate additional official parser error messages and the prepared Vitest harness installs a `toHaveBeenWarned` matcher for official warning assertions. Compiler semantics for parser/tokenizer/diagnostics remain Rust-backed.
- Latest Vue 3 core conformance state: `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue3-core` runs 652 official tests with `402` passing and `250` failing. `parse.spec.ts` is now `151/151` passing. Remaining failures are concentrated in transform/codegen suites and are outside this parser recovery slice.
- Verification for this Rust-backed parser completion slice: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo test -p vuec_html -p vuec_ast -p vuec_vue2 -p vuec_vue3_core -p vuec_node_bridge`, `cargo check -p xtask`, `cargo build -p vuec_node_bridge`, `cargo xtask verify-npm-alias --version-line vue3 --package @vue/compiler-core`, focused official-vs-Rust alias parser recovery smoke comparisons, and `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue3-core` as an expected failing full suite with `402/652` passing.

- Current round: added Rust-backed Vue 3 namespace-aware CDATA parsing for official `parse.spec.ts`. `Vue3CompilerOptions` now carries deterministic tag namespace overrides, `vuec_vue3_core::base_parse` maintains a namespace stack, projects configured SVG namespace on elements, emits CDATA body text only in XML namespace with body-only spans, and drops CDATA children in HTML namespace.
- `vuec_node_bridge` now parses the serialized namespace override map and uses the same namespace stack for CDATA diagnostics. The generated Vue 3 alias only gained an API adapter that samples function-valued `getNamespace` over tags present in the template; compiler CDATA semantics stay in Rust.
- Latest Vue 3 core conformance state: `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue3-core` runs 652 official tests with `386` passing and `266` failing. `parse.spec.ts` is now `135/151` passing with `16` failures remaining, mostly raw-text/invalid-end-tag/error-message exactness plus the `decodeEntities` warning matcher setup gap.
- Verification for this namespace/CDATA slice: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo check -p vuec_vue3_core -p vuec_node_bridge -p xtask`, `cargo test -p vuec_vue3_core -p vuec_node_bridge`, `git diff --check`, `cargo xtask verify-npm-alias --version-line vue3 --package @vue/compiler-core`, direct official-vs-Rust alias CDATA namespace smoke comparisons, and `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue3-core` as an expected failing full suite with `386/652` passing.
- Current round: tightened Rust-backed Vue 3 EOF/error-recovery parsing for official `parse.spec.ts`. `vuec_vue3_core::base_parse` now extends still-open element spans to EOF, treats an empty incomplete end tag like `</` as text under the current parent, and suppresses EOF-interrupted child start tags from becoming real AST elements while preserving recoverable trailing solidus text.
- `vuec_node_bridge` parse diagnostics now distinguishes true EOF-in-tag from an incomplete start tag interrupted by a later end tag, so the IDE recovery case keeps the official missing-end-tag diagnostic path instead of reporting a synthetic EOF_IN_TAG.
- Latest Vue 3 core conformance state: `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue3-core` runs 652 official tests with `380` passing and `272` failing. `parse.spec.ts` is now `129/151` passing with `22` failures remaining; these are concentrated in CDATA/raw-text/error-snapshot exactness plus the non-compiler `decodeEntities` warning matcher setup case.
- Verification for this Rust-backed EOF recovery slice: `cargo fmt --all --check`, `cargo check -p vuec_vue3_core -p vuec_node_bridge -p xtask`, `cargo test -p vuec_vue3_core -p vuec_html -p vuec_node_bridge`, `git diff --check`, direct official-vs-Rust alias smoke comparisons for EOF and IDE recovery inputs, and `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue3-core` as an expected failing full suite with `380/652` passing.
- Current round: extended the Rust-backed Vue 3 `baseParse` parser/diagnostic path for official parser option and recovery parity. `vuec_node_bridge` now forwards `whitespace`, `isPreTag`, and `isIgnoreNewlineTag` hits into `Vue3CompilerOptions`, and parse diagnostics now use the same `voidTags`, custom delimiters, and `v-pre` state as the Rust parser.
- `vuec_html::HtmlTokenizer` now terminates an incomplete start tag before a following end tag, so the official IDE recovery case `<template><Hello\n</template><script>...` keeps `<script>` at root level. `vuec_vue3_core::base_parse` also extends unmatched element spans to the interrupting end tag, matching the official invalid-HTML snapshot for missing end tags.
- `xtask/src/compat.rs` only changed API/hydration behavior in this round: generated Vue 3 aliases sample `isPreTag` / `isIgnoreNewlineTag` function options against tags present in the current template, and hydrate error code 24 with the official message `Element is missing end tag.`. No compiler semantics were moved into the JS shim.

- Current round: aligned `docs/2.DEVELOPMENT_PLAN.md` and `docs/goal.md` to the deterministic AST/HIR/MIR contract in `docs/3.AST_HIR_MIR_DESIGN.md`, emphasizing that `Vue2Ast`, `Vue3Ast`, `HIR`, `Vue2Mir`, `Vue3DomMir`, `Vue3SsrMir`, and `VaporMir` are first-class target documents with fixed public projection, lowering, and target-split rules.
- Active objective: build a Rust Vue compiler that can replace official Vue 2.6, Vue 2.7, and Vue 3 compiler flows.
- Current focus: use the now-real Vue 3 compiler-core official conformance runner to drive missing parser/transform/codegen compatibility while keeping implementation aligned with the deterministic AST/HIR/MIR contract.
- `cargo xtask run-conformance --suite vue3-core` now executes the official Vue 3 `packages/compiler-core/__tests__` suite through a prepared Vitest tree and generated Rust alias shims. Current result is real failure, not pending: 652 official tests run, 134 pass, 518 fail. The dominant remaining failures are transformElement/buildProps semantics, v-if/v-for codegen, expression rewriting, exact codegen snapshots, and remaining parser edge cases around whitespace, v-pre, parse-expression ASTs, and full diagnostic coverage.
- The Vue 3 core conformance runner copies official tests into `target/conformance/<lock-hash>/prepared/vue3-core`, generates `packages/compiler-core/src/*` and transform shims that load the Rust alias package plus its non-enumerable `__vuecRuntime` internal compatibility surface, and runs Vitest from the pinned npm runner install. Zero-test executions are explicitly fail, not pass.
- Vue 3 compiler-core alias now has a first public runtime compatibility layer for enum values, runtime helper symbols, `helperNameMap`, `locStub`, AST builder functions, source-position helpers, compiler errors, a JS-side transform context/walker, basic structural directive transforms, minimal core transform shims, and a minimal `generate` implementation. This removes the previous broad `transform` / `createSimpleExpression` not-implemented failure class without treating the suite as passed.
- Vue 3 parser now projects raw directive attributes such as `:class`, `@click`, `#default`, and `v-*` into `Vue3Prop::Directive` with directive name, raw name, arg, expression, modifiers, and dynamic-arg metadata before bridge hydration to official-like public AST objects.
- Vue 3 parser/projection now carries lossless attribute name/value spans and quote kind from `vuec_html::HtmlTokenizer` into `Vue3Attribute` / `Vue3Directive`, so Node bridge public AST projection emits deterministic official-style `loc`, `nameLoc`, quoted `value.loc.source`, directive `arg` / `exp` / modifier locations, and undefined hydration for absent fields. The current parser slice also preserves official directive `arg` raw bracket spans, directive `exp` whitespace, and the `.prop` synthetic modifier's empty `loc` / non-static shape in public projection instead of normalizing them away.
- Vue 3 parser options now cover the first official parse-spec set: `comments`, custom `delimiters`, `isVoidTag`, `isNativeTag`, `isCustomElement`, and `isBuiltInComponent`. Function-valued tag predicates are sampled in the generated JS alias against tags present in the current template, then passed to Rust as deterministic arrays.
- Vue 3 parse diagnostics now flow from the Rust bridge back through alias hydration to caller-provided `onError` callbacks for the implemented diagnostic subset: invalid first tag character, missing interpolation end, invalid end tag, and missing directive name. Diagnostics intentionally remain incomplete and are not marked passing globally.
- `docs/3.AST_HIR_MIR_DESIGN.md` is the authoritative AST/IR contract; `vuec_ast` now has the deterministic arena root, `NodeSpan`, `LoweringMap`, public projection scaffolding, CST structs, first-class Vue2Ast/Vue3Ast/HIR schema structs, HIR without runtime helper/codegen call variants, target-split MIR enums, runtime-helper enum, and JS id model.
- `vuec_ast` now exposes concrete Vue 2 AST structs (`Vue2Root`, `Vue2Element`, `Vue2Text`, `Vue2ExpressionText`, `Vue2Comment`, Vue 2 filter/model/directive/event structs) and concrete Vue 3 AST structs (`Vue3Root`, `Vue3Element`, `Vue3Text`, `Vue3Comment`, `Vue3Interpolation`, `Vue3CompoundExpression`, `Vue3If`, `Vue3IfBranch`, `Vue3For`, `Vue3TextCall`) while keeping compatibility constructors for existing call sites.
- `vuec_vue3_core`, `vuec_vue3_dom`, `vuec_vue3_ssr`, `vuec_vue2`, and `vuec_node_bridge` have been migrated to the new tuple-variant AST schema without changing current public compiler behavior.
- Vue 3 `TemplateSource.base_offset` now correctly maps closing-tag-updated element spans back to absolute original-file offsets; a regression test covers a template block starting at offset 42.
- `vuec_js` now has the first real registry/store layer: `JsEntry`, `JsSourceType`, id allocation for expressions/statements/patterns/programs, lookup APIs, and parse-by-id APIs while preserving the old direct parse wrappers.
- Vue 3 `@vue/compiler-core` now validates interpolation expressions through `vuec_js::JsAstStore` and Oxc, with `isTS` / `expressionPlugins: ["typescript"]` selecting TypeScript expression parsing.
- Vue 3 `@vue/compiler-core` option matrix now has all 8 schema v2 rows passing for the current fixtures: prefixIdentifiers, mode, hoistStatic, cacheHandlers, scopeId, slotted, isTS, and expressionPlugins.
- Vue 3 core now mirrors official `cacheHandlers` / `scopeId` invalid option-combination errors through the generated npm alias preflight layer, lowers `<slot>` to `renderSlot`, and emits the current static-child cache signal for `hoistStatic`.
- `vuec_sfc::compile_script` now registers normal script and script setup blocks as `JsProgramId`s and serializes official-style `type`, `setup`, `lang`, `imports`, `scriptAst`, `scriptSetupAst`, and `deps` fields.
- Vue 3 SFC option matrix now has parse, compileTemplate, compileScript, and compileStyle executable rows passing for the current schema v2 fixtures.
- Vue 3 `@vue/compiler-ssr` option matrix now has scopeId and slotted executable rows passing for the current schema v2 fixtures.
- Vue 3 `@vue/compiler-dom` option matrix now has prefixIdentifiers, transformAssetUrls, decodeEntities, and isCustomElement executable rows passing for the current schema v2 fixtures.
- Vue 2.7 `vue/compiler-sfc` now uses version-specific npm alias bridge commands and has parse, compileTemplate, compileScript, and compileStyle option rows passing for the current schema v2 fixtures.
- Vue 2.7 `vue/compiler-sfc` output-contract probing now uses the official 2.7 single-object `parse({ source, filename })` API instead of the Vue 3 `parse(source, options)` call shape; `cargo xtask run-output-contract --all` has no failing checks and all seven targets are now `5/5`, with runtime parity executed directly in the probe.
- Vue 2.7 `vue-template-compiler` option matrix now has warn, modules, and directives executable rows passing for the current schema v2 fixtures.
- Vue 2.6 `vue-template-compiler` option matrix now has all 10 schema v2 rows passing, including `outputSourceRange` on the malformed tag fixture.
- Vue 2 parser recovery now closes through a matching end tag and reports the unmatched intermediate element once, matching the current Vue 2.6 `outputSourceRange` fixture. Vue 2 compile defaults now enable static optimization like the official compiler.
- Vue 2 public compile projection now classifies compiler warnings into official `errors`, serializes `errors` / `tips` as string arrays unless `outputSourceRange` is enabled, and hides internal `diagnostics` from the npm alias surface.
- `vuec_ast` now carries the new base arena shape from the AST/HIR/MIR design: `AstDocument.root` is a required `NodeId`, parent/index tracking is validated by `validate_tree`, `NodeSpan::Generated` / `Missing` have explicit constructors, `LoweringMap` has explicit AST->HIR and HIR->MIR edge APIs, and generic nested `PublicProjection` is available for deterministic snapshots.
- `vuec_ast` no longer exposes the old generic `MirNodeKind` model. MIR is split into `Vue2Mir`, `Vue3DomMir`, `Vue3SsrMir`, and `VaporMir`, wrapped by `Mir` with a target discriminator so downstream codegen can be made target-specific.
- `vuec_pass` now uses `RuntimeHelper` enums and exposes a depth-first `DocumentPass` walker over `AstDocument`, so the transform layer can align with the new IR plan.
- Vue 3 SFC work is now past the skeleton stage: template compilation keeps raw source for official-style output and source maps, module-mode render codegen emits direct Vue imports, and side-effect `<script>/<style>` handling is AST/diagnostic-driven.
- Remaining Vue 3 parity work is broader output-contract and official test execution coverage beyond the current option-matrix fixtures, including exact AST/codegen shape where the matrix only checks a deterministic compatibility signal.
- Compatibility rule: Vue 2.7 SFC export is `vue/compiler-sfc`, not a standalone `@vue/compiler-sfc@2.7.x` package.
- Workspace now has initial crates for `vuec_source`, `vuec_diagnostics`, `vuec_html`, `vuec_js`, `vuec_ast`, `vuec_pass`, `vuec_codegen`, plus an `xtask` compatibility harness scaffold.
- Workspace now also has initial `vuec_sfc` and `vuec_vue3_core` crates with compileable public interfaces and smoke tests.
- Added `vuec_vue3_dom`, `vuec_vue3_ssr`, and `vuec_style` crates. These are functional early backends with unit tests, not official parity-complete implementations.
- `vuec_sfc` now routes `compileTemplate` through DOM or SSR backends, routes `compileStyle` through `vuec_style`, and uses Oxc-backed `vuec_js` program summaries for script bindings/errors.
- `xtask sync-official-tests --locked` now performs real git checkout of the official Vue 2.6 / 2.7 / 3 repositories pinned by `compat/official-revisions.lock`.
- `xtask run-conformance --all` now discovers official test files, checks Rust alias package readiness, provisions deterministic official runner dependencies from the synced official lock/manifests, and executes per-suite alias smoke calls through the generated bridge. All seven suites are runner-ready; full official spec execution is still pending because the harness has not yet invoked the official Vitest/Jasmine specs through the alias package setup.
- Runner dependency provisioning is lock-aware: Vue 2.6 installs `@babel/register@7.0.0` and `jasmine@2.99.0` from `yarn.lock`; Vue 2.7 installs Vitest/esbuild/TypeScript from its `pnpm-lock.yaml`; Vue 3 installs Vitest/esbuild/TypeScript/source-map-js from its `pnpm-lock.yaml`.
- `vuec_vue2` has moved beyond the skeleton: it now has a recursive Vue 2 element AST, directive parsing for core Vue 2 directives, filter rewriting, static optimization, official-style render/staticRenderFns codegen, event handler generation, model helpers, diagnostics, and Vue 2 codeframe generation. It is still not official parity-complete.
- `vuec_html` now accepts Vue directive attribute names such as `@click.stop`, `#default`, `.prop`, and dynamic-argument syntax without tokenizer stalls.
- `xtask --version-line` now accepts the canonical plan values `vue2_6`, `vue2_7`, and `vue3` while retaining `vue26` / `vue27` aliases.
- `cargo xtask export-api --all` now installs exact official npm versions from `compat/official-revisions.lock`, generates Rust npm alias packages under `target/compat/rust-alias/<version-line>`, probes both sides with Node, and writes normalized API manifests for all seven compiler targets.
- `cargo xtask diff-api --all` now passes for all seven compiler targets at API export/arity/type-declaration shape level, using `compat/api/allowed-diff.json` for explicit approved differences.
- `cargo xtask verify-npm-alias --all` now builds the `vuec_node_bridge` JSON bridge, generates alias packages, requires each official package name, and smoke-calls a representative Rust-backed compiler entry for each target. This is a development bridge, not the final NAPI package.
- `cargo xtask run-option-matrix --all` now executes real official-vs-Rust npm alias probes and writes `target/conformance/<lock-hash>/option-matrix.json`; all seven current option-matrix targets pass with no pending or failing rows.
- `cargo xtask run-output-contract --all` now executes real official-vs-Rust npm alias probes and writes `target/conformance/<lock-hash>/output-contract.json`; schema, exact JS output, diagnostics, source-map, and runtime parity checks have no failing rows across all seven targets.
- `cargo xtask summarize-compat --locked` now reads real API, option, output, conformance, and lock artifacts instead of returning a scaffolded summary. Current summary is `pending` only because official conformance reports still have pending full-spec execution; API, option, output, lock, and conformance alias smoke statuses are `pass`.
- Vue 2 newline option handling now parses `shouldDecodeNewlines` / `shouldDecodeNewlinesForHref` through the Node bridge and emits href newline escapes compatible with the Vue 2.6 option probe.
